Polynomial Speedup in Diffusion Models with the Multilevel Euler-Maruyama Method
Authors: Arthur Jacot
First: 2026-03-25T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:59:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the Multilevel Euler-Maruyama (ML-EM) method compute solutions of SDEs and ODEs using a range of approximators $f^1,\dots,f^k$ to the drift $f$ with increasing accuracy and computational cost, only requiring a few evaluations of the most accurate $f^k$ and many evaluations of the less costly $f^1,\dots,f^{k-1}$. If the drift lies in the so-called Harder than Monte Carlo (HTMC) regime, i.e. it requires $ε^{-γ}$ compute to be $ε$-approximated for some $γ>2$, then ML-EM $ε$-approximates the solution of the SDE with $ε^{-γ}$ compute, improving over the traditional EM rate of $ε^{-γ-1}$. In other terms it allows us to solve the SDE at the same cost as a single evaluation of the drift. In the context of diffusion models, the different levels $f^{1},\dots,f^{k}$ are obtained by training UNets of increasing sizes, and ML-EM allows us to perform sampling with the equivalent of a single evaluation of the largest UNet. Our numerical experiments confirm our theory: we obtain up to fourfold speedups for image generation on the CelebA dataset downscaled to 64x64, where we measure a $γ\approx2.5$. Given that this is a polynomial speedup, we expect even stronger speedups in practical applications which involve orders of magnitude larger networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用多层欧拉-马鲁亚玛方法在扩散模型中实现多项式加速
我们引入多层欧拉-马鲁亚玛（ML-EM）方法，通过一系列精度递增、计算成本递增的漂移项近似器 $f^1,\dots,f^k$ 计算随机微分方程（SDE）和常微分方程（ODE）的解，仅需少量最精确的 $f^k$ 评估，而大量评估成本较低的 $f^1,\dots,f^{k-1}$。若漂移项处于所谓“比蒙特卡罗更难”（HTMC）区域，即需 $ε^{-γ}$ 计算量才能实现 $ε$ 精度近似（其中 $γ>2$），则 ML-EM 可以用 $ε^{-γ}$ 计算量实现 SDE 解的 $ε$ 精度近似，优于传统 EM 方法的 $ε^{-γ-1}$ 精度。换言之，它允许我们以单次漂移项评估的计算成本解决 SDE。在扩散模型背景下，不同层级 $f^{1},\dots,f^{k}$ 通过训练尺寸递增的 UNet 获得，ML-EM 使我们能够以相当于单次最大 UNet 评估的代价进行采样。我们的数值实验验证了理论：在将 CelebA 数据集下采样至 64x64 的图像生成任务中，我们实现了最高四倍的加速，其中 $γ\approx2.5$。鉴于这是多项式加速，我们预期在涉及规模大得多网络的实际应用中，加速效果会更强。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of drift evaluations in diffusion models, especially in the Harder than Monte Carlo regime, this paper proposes the Multilevel Euler-Maruyama (ML-EM) method, which combines multiple approximations of the drift function with varying accuracy and cost. By leveraging cheap, coarse approximations for most of the computation and only using the most accurate one sparingly, ML-EM achieves a polynomial speedup in sampling. Experimental results on 64x64 CelebA images confirm the theoretical prediction: with γ≈2.5, the method delivers up to fourfold speedup compared to standard Euler-Maruyama, effectively reducing the cost of sampling to that of a single evaluation of the largest UNet.
为降低扩散模型中高精度漂移项计算的高昂开销，本文提出多层级欧拉-马鲁亚玛（ML-EM）方法，通过组合多个精度与成本递增的近似器，仅需少量高精度近似器计算，而大量使用低成本近似器。当漂移项处于“比蒙特卡洛更难”（HTMC）区域（需ε^{-γ}计算量以实现ε精度，γ>2）时，ML-EM可实现ε精度下的ε^{-γ}计算量，优于传统欧拉-马鲁亚玛方法的ε^{-γ-1}速率。在扩散模型中，该方法通过不同规模的UNet架构层级实现，等效于仅使用最大UNet一次评估。在64x64 CelebA数据集实验中，γ≈2.5时获得最高四倍加速，预示在实际应用中（尤其涉及更大网络时）可实现更显著的加速效果。
Vibe Coding XR: Accelerating AI + XR Prototyping with XR Blocks and Gemini
Authors: Ruofei Du, Benjamin Hersh, David Li, Nels Numan, Xun Qian, Yanhe Chen, Zhongyi Zhou, Xingyue Chen, Jiahao Ren, Robert Timothy Bettridge, Steve Toh, David Kim
First: 2026-03-25T17:58:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:58:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
While large language models have accelerated software development through "vibe coding", prototyping intelligent Extended Reality (XR) experiences remains inaccessible due to the friction of complex game engines and low-level sensor integration. To bridge this gap, we contribute XR Blocks, an open-source, modular WebXR framework that abstracts spatial computing complexities into high-level, human-centered primitives. Building upon this foundation, we present Vibe Coding XR, an end-to-end rapid prototyping workflow that leverages LLMs to translate natural language intent directly into functional XR software. Using a web-based interface, creators can transform high-level prompts (e.g., "create a dandelion that reacts to hand") into interactive WebXR applications in under a minute. We provide a preliminary technical evaluation on a pilot dataset (VCXR60) alongside diverse application scenarios highlighting mixed-reality realism, multi-modal interaction, and generative AI integrations. By democratizing spatial software creation, this work empowers practitioners to bypass low-level hurdles and rapidly move from "idea to reality." Code and live demos are available at https://xrblocks.github.io/gem and https://github.com/google/xrblocks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Vibe Coding XR：利用XR Blocks与Gemini加速AI+XR原型开发
尽管大型语言模型通过“氛围编码”加速了软件开发，但智能扩展现实（XR）体验的原型设计仍因复杂的游戏引擎和底层传感器集成而难以实现。为弥合这一差距，我们贡献了XR Blocks，一个开源、模块化的WebXR框架，将空间计算的复杂性抽象为高层次、以人为中心的原语。在此基础上，我们提出了Vibe Coding XR，一个端到端的快速原型开发工作流，利用LLM将自然语言意图直接转化为功能性的XR软件。通过基于Web的界面，创作者可在一分钟内将高层次提示（如“创建一个随手势反应的蒲公英”）转化为交互式WebXR应用。我们对试点数据集（VCXR60）进行了初步技术评估，并展示了多种应用场景，涵盖混合现实真实感、多模态交互和生成式AI集成。通过民主化空间软件创作，本工作使从业者能够绕过底层障碍，快速实现“从想法到现实”。代码和实时演示可在https://xrblocks.github.io/gem和https://github.com/google/xrblocks获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between LLM-driven "vibe coding" and the complexity of XR prototyping, this work introduces XR Blocks, an open-source WebXR framework that simplifies spatial computing through human-centered primitives. Building on XR Blocks, Vibe Coding XR enables users to rapidly prototype XR applications by translating natural language prompts into functional WebXR code via a web interface. Experimental results on a pilot dataset (VCXR60) demonstrate that users can generate interactive XR experiences in under a minute, showcasing capabilities in mixed-reality realism, multi-modal interaction, and generative AI integration, thereby lowering barriers to entry for spatial software creation.
为降低非专业用户构建XR原型的门槛，本研究提出XR Blocks，一个将复杂空间计算抽象为直观、以人为本的模块化WebXR框架。在此基础上，Vibe Coding XR允许用户通过自然语言指令（如“创建一个随手势反应的蒲公英”）在网页界面中快速生成可交互的WebXR应用，全程不到一分钟。基于试点数据集（VCXR60）及多样应用场景的实验表明，该系统在实现混合现实真实感、多模态交互与生成式AI集成方面效果显著，大幅降低了空间软件开发的准入门槛。
DreamerAD: Efficient Reinforcement Learning via Latent World Model for Autonomous Driving
Authors: Pengxuan Yang, Yupeng Zheng, Deheng Qian, Zebin Xing, Qichao Zhang, Linbo Wang, Yichen Zhang, Shaoyu Guo, Zhongpu Xia, Qiang Chen, Junyu Han, Lingyun Xu, Yifeng Pan, Dongbin Zhao
First: 2026-03-25T17:57:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:57:09+00:00
Comments: first version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce DreamerAD, the first latent world model framework that enables efficient reinforcement learning for autonomous driving by compressing diffusion sampling from 100 steps to 1 - achieving 80x speedup while maintaining visual interpretability. Training RL policies on real-world driving data incurs prohibitive costs and safety risks. While existing pixel-level diffusion world models enable safe imagination-based training, they suffer from multi-step diffusion inference latency (2s/frame) that prevents high-frequency RL interaction. Our approach leverages denoised latent features from video generation models through three key mechanisms: (1) shortcut forcing that reduces sampling complexity via recursive multi-resolution step compression, (2) an autoregressive dense reward model operating directly on latent representations for fine-grained credit assignment, and (3) Gaussian vocabulary sampling for GRPO that constrains exploration to physically plausible trajectories. DreamerAD achieves 87.7 EPDMS on NavSim v2, establishing state-of-the-art performance and demonstrating that latent-space RL is effective for autonomous driving.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DreamerAD：通过潜在世界模型实现自动驾驶的高效强化学习
我们提出了DreamerAD，这是首个通过将扩散采样从100步压缩至1步，实现80倍加速同时保持视觉可解释性的潜在世界模型框架，用于自动驾驶的高效强化学习。在真实驾驶数据上训练强化学习策略成本高昂且存在安全风险。虽然现有的像素级扩散世界模型支持基于想象的安全训练，但其多步扩散推理延迟（每帧2秒）阻碍了高频强化学习交互。我们的方法通过三个关键机制利用视频生成模型的去噪潜在特征：（1）捷径强制，通过递归多分辨率步长压缩降低采样复杂度；（2）在潜在表示上直接操作的自回归密集奖励模型，实现精细的信用分配；（3）高斯词汇采样用于GRPO，将探索约束在物理上可行的轨迹内。DreamerAD在NavSim v2上达到87.7 EPDMS，创下当前最优性能，证明了潜在空间强化学习在自动驾驶中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and safety risks of training RL policies on real-world driving data, DreamerAD introduces a latent world model framework that compresses diffusion sampling from 100 steps to just one, achieving an 80x speedup while preserving visual interpretability. It leverages denoised latent features from video generation models via three mechanisms: shortcut forcing for recursive multi-resolution step compression, an autoregressive dense reward model for fine-grained credit assignment in latent space, and Gaussian vocabulary sampling to constrain exploration to physically plausible trajectories. Experiments show DreamerAD achieves 87.7 EPDMS on NavSim v2, setting a new state-of-the-art performance for autonomous driving via latent-space RL.
为降低真实驾驶数据训练RL策略的成本与安全风险，DreamerAD提出首个基于潜在世界模型的框架，将扩散采样步数从100压缩至1，实现80倍加速且保持视觉可解释性。其核心机制包括：通过递归多分辨率步压缩的快捷路径强制采样、直接在潜在表示上运行的自回归密集奖励模型以实现精细的信用分配、以及高斯词汇采样约束探索轨迹的物理合理性。在NavSim v2上，该方法达到87.7 EPDMS，创下自动驾驶领域基于潜在空间RL的最新性能纪录。
Comparing Developer and LLM Biases in Code Evaluation
Authors: Aditya Mittal, Ryan Shar, Zichu Wu, Shyam Agarwal, Tongshuang Wu, Chris Donahue, Ameet Talwalkar, Wayne Chi, Valerie Chen
First: 2026-03-25T17:56:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:56:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As LLMs are increasingly used as judges in code applications, they should be evaluated in realistic interactive settings that capture partial context and ambiguous intent. We present TRACE (Tool for Rubric Analysis in Code Evaluation), a framework that evaluates LLM judges' ability to predict human preferences and automatically extracts rubric items to reveal systematic biases in how humans and models weigh each item. Across three modalities -- chat-based programming, IDE autocompletion, and instructed code editing -- we use TRACE to measure how well LLM judges align with developer preferences. Among 13 different models, the best judges underperform human annotators by 12-23%. TRACE identifies 35 significant sources of misalignment between humans and judges across interaction modalities, the majority of which correspond to existing software engineering code quality criteria. For example, in chat-based coding, judges are biased towards longer code explanations while humans prefer shorter ones. We find significant misalignment on the majority of existing code quality dimensions, showing alignment gaps between LLM judges and human preference in realistic coding applications.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate LLMs as code judges in realistic settings, the study introduces TRACE, a framework that measures how well LLMs predict human preferences in code evaluation and automatically extracts rubric items to reveal biases. Across three interaction modalities — chat-based programming, IDE autocompletion, and instructed code editing — TRACE compares 13 LLMs against human annotators, finding that even the best models underperform humans by 12–23%. The framework identifies 35 key misalignment sources, most aligned with established software engineering quality criteria, such as LLMs favoring longer code explanations while humans prefer brevity, highlighting systematic gaps in how LLMs and humans weigh code quality dimensions.
为评估大语言模型在真实交互场景中作为代码评判者的有效性，研究提出TRACE框架，用于衡量模型预测人类偏好能力并自动提取评分标准以揭示偏见。在聊天编程、IDE自动补全和指令式代码编辑三种交互模式下，TRACE对比13个模型与人类标注者的表现，发现最优模型仍比人类低12–23%。框架识别出35个显著的人机偏好不一致来源，多数对应软件工程中的质量标准，例如模型偏好长代码解释而人类更倾向简洁，表明在多数代码质量维度上，大模型与人类偏好存在显著差距。
TAG: Target-Agnostic Guidance for Stable Object-Centric Inference in Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Jiaying Zhou, Zhihao Zhan, Ruifeng Zhai, Qinhan Lyu, Hao Liu, Keze Wang, Liang Lin, Guangrun Wang
First: 2026-03-25T17:56:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:56:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision--Language--Action (VLA) policies have shown strong progress in mapping language instructions and visual observations to robotic actions, yet their reliability degrades in cluttered scenes with distractors. By analyzing failure cases, we find that many errors do not arise from infeasible motions, but from instance-level grounding failures: the policy often produces a plausible grasp trajectory that lands slightly off-target or even on the wrong object instance. To address this issue, we propose TAG (Target-Agnostic Guidance), a simple inference-time guidance mechanism that explicitly reduces distractor- and appearance-induced bias in VLA policies. Inspired by classifier-free guidance (CFG), TAG contrasts policy predictions under the original observation and an object-erased observation, and uses their difference as a residual steering signal that strengthens the influence of object evidence in the decision process. TAG does not require modifying the policy architecture and can be integrated with existing VLA policies with minimal training and inference changes. We evaluate TAG on standard manipulation benchmarks, including LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and VLABench, where it consistently improves robustness under clutter and reduces near-miss and wrong-object executions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TAG：面向视觉-语言-动作模型中稳定以物体为中心推理的目标无关引导
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在将语言指令和视觉观测映射到机器人动作方面取得了显著进展，但在存在干扰物的杂乱场景中其可靠性会下降。通过分析失败案例，我们发现许多错误并非源于不可行的动作，而是源于实例级定位失败：策略常生成一个看似合理的抓取轨迹，但最终落在目标物体附近或错误的物体实例上。为解决此问题，我们提出TAG（目标无关引导），一种简单的推理时引导机制，可显式减少VLA策略中由干扰物和外观引起的偏差。受无分类器引导（CFG）启发，TAG对比原始观测与物体擦除观测下的策略预测，并利用其差异作为残差引导信号，增强物体证据在决策过程中的影响。TAG无需修改策略架构，可与现有VLA策略无缝集成，仅需极小的训练和推理调整。我们在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和VLABench等标准操作基准上评估TAG，结果表明其在杂乱环境下的鲁棒性持续提升，并显著减少近失和错误物体执行。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that VLA policies often fail in cluttered scenes not due to motion infeasibility but due to mis-grounding of target objects, the authors propose TAG, a target-agnostic guidance mechanism that enhances object-centric inference. TAG leverages a contrastive approach inspired by classifier-free guidance, comparing policy outputs under original and object-erased visual inputs to generate a residual steering signal that prioritizes object evidence. Evaluated on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and VLABench, TAG consistently improves robustness to clutter and reduces both near-miss and wrong-object grasp executions without requiring architectural changes to existing VLA policies.
鉴于视觉-语言-动作模型在杂乱场景中常因目标实例定位错误而非运动不可行而失败，作者提出TAG（目标无关引导）机制，通过对比原始与去目标视觉输入下的策略输出，生成残差引导信号以强化目标证据的影响。该方法无需修改模型架构，可无缝集成现有VLA策略。在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和VLABench等基准测试中，TAG显著提升对杂乱环境的鲁棒性，有效减少近目标误抓与错物抓取事件。
The Stochastic Gap: A Markovian Framework for Pre-Deployment Reliability and Oversight-Cost Auditing in Agentic Artificial Intelligence
Authors: Biplab Pal, Santanu Bhattacharya
First: 2026-03-25T17:56:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:56:11+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 5 figures, submitted to Engineering Applications of Artificial Intelligence
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) in organizations is a sequential decision problem constrained by reliability and oversight cost. When deterministic workflows are replaced by stochastic policies over actions and tool calls, the key question is not whether a next step appears plausible, but whether the resulting trajectory remains statistically supported, locally unambiguous, and economically governable. We develop a measure-theoretic Markov framework for this setting. The core quantities are state blind-spot mass B_n(tau), state-action blind mass B^SA_{pi,n}(tau), an entropy-based human-in-the-loop escalation gate, and an expected oversight-cost identity over the workflow visitation measure. We instantiate the framework on the Business Process Intelligence Challenge 2019 purchase-to-pay log (251,734 cases, 1,595,923 events, 42 distinct workflow actions) and construct a log-driven simulated agent from a chronological 80/20 split of the same process. The main empirical finding is that a large workflow can appear well supported at the state level while retaining substantial blind mass over next-step decisions: refining the operational state to include case context, economic magnitude, and actor class expands the state space from 42 to 668 and raises state-action blind mass from 0.0165 at tau=50 to 0.1253 at tau=1000. On the held-out split, m(s) = max_a pi-hat(a|s) tracks realized autonomous step accuracy within 3.4 percentage points on average. The same quantities that delimit statistically credible autonomy also determine expected oversight burden. The framework is demonstrated on a large-scale enterprise procurement workflow and is designed for direct application to engineering processes for which operational event logs are available.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess reliability and oversight costs in stochastic agentic AI systems, the paper introduces a measure-theoretic Markov framework that quantifies blind mass in state and state-action spaces to evaluate statistical support and economic governability of trajectories. Applied to a large procurement workflow log, the framework reveals that even well-supported states can exhibit substantial blind mass in next-step decisions, which increases significantly when operational context is enriched. Experimental results show that refining the state space from 42 to 668 actions improves blind mass from 0.0165 to 0.1253 over 1000 steps, while a simulated agent’s step accuracy closely tracks real-world performance within 3.4 percentage points. The same metrics also predict oversight burden, enabling direct application to real-world engineering processes with event logs.
为评估随机化智能体系统中的可靠性与监督成本，本文提出一种测度论马尔可夫框架，通过状态与状态-动作盲区质量量化轨迹的统计支持度与经济可治理性。在大型采购流程日志上实验发现，即使状态支持度高，下一步决策仍可能保留大量盲区，且当状态空间从42扩展至668（加入案例上下文、经济规模与执行者类别）时，盲区质量从0.0165升至0.1253。模拟智能体的步骤准确率与真实表现平均偏差仅3.4个百分点，且同一框架可预测监督负担，适用于具备操作日志的工程流程直接部署。
Latent-WAM: Latent World Action Modeling for End-to-End Autonomous Driving
Authors: Linbo Wang, Yupeng Zheng, Qiang Chen, Shiwei Li, Yichen Zhang, Zebin Xing, Qichao Zhang, Xiang Li, Deheng Qian, Pengxuan Yang, Yihang Dong, Ce Hao, Xiaoqing Ye, Junyu han, Yifeng Pan, Dongbin Zhao
First: 2026-03-25T17:56:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:56:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Latent-WAM, an efficient end-to-end autonomous driving framework that achieves strong trajectory planning through spatially-aware and dynamics-informed latent world representations. Existing world-model-based planners suffer from inadequately compressed representations, limited spatial understanding, and underutilized temporal dynamics, resulting in sub-optimal planning under constrained data and compute budgets. Latent-WAM addresses these limitations with two core modules: a Spatial-Aware Compressive World Encoder (SCWE) that distills geometric knowledge from a foundation model and compresses multi-view images into compact scene tokens via learnable queries, and a Dynamic Latent World Model (DLWM) that employs a causal Transformer to autoregressively predict future world status conditioned on historical visual and motion representations. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM v2 and HUGSIM demonstrate new state-of-the-art results: 89.3 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 and 28.9 HD-Score on HUGSIM, surpassing the best prior perception-free method by 3.2 EPDMS with significantly less training data and a compact 104M-parameter model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Latent-WAM：面向端到端自动驾驶的潜在世界动作建模
我们提出了Latent-WAM，一种高效的端到端自动驾驶框架，通过空间感知且动态信息驱动的潜在世界表示实现强大的轨迹规划。现有基于世界模型的规划器因表示压缩不足、空间理解有限及时间动态利用不充分，在受限的数据和计算预算下导致规划次优。Latent-WAM通过两个核心模块解决这些问题：空间感知压缩世界编码器（SCWE），从基础模型中蒸馏几何知识，通过可学习查询将多视角图像压缩为紧凑场景令牌；动态潜在世界模型（DLWM）采用因果Transformer，基于历史视觉与运动表示自回归预测未来世界状态。在NAVSIM v2和HUGSIM上的大量实验表明，该方法取得新SOTA结果：NAVSIM v2上89.3 EPDMS，HUGSIM上28.9 HD-Score，相比最佳先前无感知方法提升3.2 EPDMS，且训练数据更少、模型仅1.04亿参数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiencies of existing world-model-based planners in autonomous driving—such as poor representation compression, limited spatial understanding, and underused temporal dynamics—Latent-WAM introduces a two-module framework: a Spatial-Aware Compressive World Encoder (SCWE) that distills geometric knowledge and compresses multi-view images into scene tokens, and a Dynamic Latent World Model (DLWM) that uses a causal Transformer to predict future world states. Experiments on NAVSIM v2 and HUGSIM show the method achieves 89.3 EPDMS and 28.9 HD-Score, outperforming the best prior perception-free method by 3.2 EPDMS while using significantly less training data and a compact 104M-parameter model.
为解决现有基于世界模型的自动驾驶规划器在表示压缩不足、空间理解有限及时间动态利用不够等问题，Latent-WAM 提出双模块架构：空间感知压缩世界编码器（SCWE）通过基础模型提取几何知识，将多视角图像压缩为场景令牌；动态潜藏世界模型（DLWM）则采用因果Transformer自回归预测未来世界状态。在NAVSIM v2和HUGSIM上的实验表明，该方法取得89.3 EPDMS和28.9 HD-Score，超越最佳无感知方法3.2 EPDMS，同时训练数据更少、模型仅104M参数。
Retrieval Improvements Do Not Guarantee Better Answers: A Study of RAG for AI Policy QA
Authors: Saahil Mathur, Ryan David Rittner, Vedant Ajit Thakur, Daniel Stuart Schiff, Tunazzina Islam
First: 2026-03-25T17:54:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:54:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are increasingly used to analyze complex policy documents, but achieving sufficient reliability for expert usage remains challenging in domains characterized by dense legal language and evolving, overlapping regulatory frameworks. We study the application of RAG to AI governance and policy analysis using the AI Governance and Regulatory Archive (AGORA) corpus, a curated collection of 947 AI policy documents. Our system combines a ColBERT-based retriever fine-tuned with contrastive learning and a generator aligned to human preferences using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). We construct synthetic queries and collect pairwise preferences to adapt the system to the policy domain. Through experiments evaluating retrieval quality, answer relevance, and faithfulness, we find that domain-specific fine-tuning improves retrieval metrics but does not consistently improve end-to-end question answering performance. In some cases, stronger retrieval counterintuitively leads to more confident hallucinations when relevant documents are absent from the corpus. These results highlight a key concern for those building policy-focused RAG systems: improvements to individual components do not necessarily translate to more reliable answers. Our findings provide practical insights for designing grounded question-answering systems over dynamic regulatory corpora.
中文标题/摘要
标题：检索改进并不保证更优答案：一项针对AI政策问答的RAG研究
检索增强生成（RAG）系统正越来越多地用于分析复杂的政策文件，但在法律语言密集且监管框架不断演变、重叠的领域中，实现专家级使用的充分可靠性仍具挑战。我们利用AI治理与监管档案（AGORA）语料库——包含947份AI政策文件——研究RAG在AI治理与政策分析中的应用。系统结合了基于ColBERT的检索器（通过对比学习微调）和与人类偏好对齐的生成器（使用直接偏好优化DPO）。我们构建合成查询并收集成对偏好，以适应政策领域。通过评估检索质量、答案相关性与忠实度的实验，我们发现领域特定微调可提升检索指标，但并不总能提升端到端问答性能。在某些情况下，更强的检索反而在语料库中缺乏相关文档时导致更自信的幻觉。这些结果凸显了构建政策导向RAG系统者的关键关切：单个组件的改进未必能转化为更可靠的答案。我们的研究为设计面向动态监管语料库的基于事实的问答系统提供了实用洞见。
Summary / 总结
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are increasingly used to analyze complex policy documents, but achieving sufficient reliability for expert usage remains challenging in domains characterized by dense legal language and evolving, overlapping regulatory frameworks.
鉴于在复杂法律语言和不断演变的监管框架下，基于检索增强生成（RAG）的系统难以实现可靠的AI政策问答，本研究在AI治理与监管档案（AGORA）语料库（含947份AI政策文件）上评估了RAG系统。该系统采用对比学习微调的ColBERT检索器和基于直接偏好优化（DPO）对齐人类偏好的生成器，并通过合成查询和人类偏好数据进行训练。实验表明，领域特定微调虽提升检索指标，但未稳定提升端到端问答质量，甚至在相关文档缺失时，更强的检索反而导致更自信的幻觉，说明组件级改进未必带来整体性能提升。
MARCH: Multi-Agent Reinforced Self-Check for LLM Hallucination
Authors: Zhuo Li, Yupeng Zhang, Pengyu Cheng, Jiajun Song, Mengyu Zhou, Hao Li, Shujie Hu, Yu Qin, Erchao Zhao, Xiaoxi Jiang, Guanjun Jiang
First: 2026-03-25T17:54:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:54:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Hallucination remains a critical bottleneck for large language models (LLMs), undermining their reliability in real-world applications, especially in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems. While existing hallucination detection methods employ LLM-as-a-judge to verify LLM outputs against retrieved evidence, they suffer from inherent confirmation bias, where the verifier inadvertently reproduces the errors of the original generation. To address this, we introduce Multi-Agent Reinforced Self-Check for Hallucination (MARCH), a framework that enforces rigorous factual alignment by leveraging deliberate information asymmetry. MARCH orchestrates a collaborative pipeline of three specialized agents: a Solver, a Proposer, and a Checker. The Solver generates an initial RAG response, which the Proposer decomposes into claim-level verifiable atomic propositions. Crucially, the Checker validates these propositions against retrieved evidence in isolation, deprived of the Solver's original output. This well-crafted information asymmetry scheme breaks the cycle of self-confirmation bias. By training this pipeline with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), we enable the agents to co-evolve and optimize factual adherence. Extensive experiments across hallucination benchmarks demonstrate that MARCH substantially reduces hallucination rates. Notably, an 8B-parameter LLM equipped with MARCH achieves performance competitive with powerful closed-source models. MARCH paves a scalable path for factual self-improvement of LLMs through co-evolution. The code is at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/MARCH.
Summary / 总结
To combat hallucination in large language models—especially in RAG systems—MARCH introduces a multi-agent framework that breaks confirmation bias by enforcing information asymmetry: a Solver generates an answer, a Proposer breaks it into atomic claims, and a Checker validates each claim independently without seeing the original output. Trained via multi-agent reinforcement learning, the agents co-evolve to enhance factual alignment. Experiments show MARCH significantly reduces hallucination rates, with an 8B-parameter model achieving performance comparable to closed-source models.
为解决大语言模型在RAG系统中的幻觉问题，MARCH提出一种多智能体框架，通过信息不对称机制打破确认偏误：生成器输出答案，分解器将其拆解为原子命题，验证器独立验证每个命题而不参考原始输出。通过多智能体强化学习训练，各智能体协同进化以提升事实一致性。实验表明，MARCH显著降低幻觉率，8B参数模型表现可媲美闭源模型。
Vision-Language Models vs Human: Perceptual Image Quality Assessment
Authors: Imran Mehmood, Imad Ali Shah, Ming Ronnier Luo, Brian Deegan
First: 2026-03-25T17:54:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:54:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Psychophysical experiments remain the most reliable approach for perceptual image quality assessment (IQA), yet their cost and limited scalability encourage automated approaches. We investigate whether Vision Language Models (VLMs) can approximate human perceptual judgments across three image quality scales: contrast, colorfulness and overall preference. Six VLMs four proprietary and two openweight models are benchmarked against psychophysical data. This work presents a systematic benchmark of VLMs for perceptual IQA through comparison with human psychophysical data. The results reveal strong attribute dependent variability models with high human alignment for colorfulness (ρup to 0.93) underperform on contrast and vice-versa. Attribute weighting analysis further shows that most VLMs assign higher weights to colorfulness compared to contrast when evaluating overall preference similar to the psychophysical data. Intramodel consistency analysis reveals a counterintuitive tradeoff: the most self consistent models are not necessarily the most human aligned suggesting response variability reflects sensitivity to scene dependent perceptual cues. Furthermore, human-VLM agreement is increased with perceptual separability, indicating VLMs are more reliable when stimulus differences are clearly expressed.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉-语言模型与人类：感知图像质量评估
心理物理学实验仍是感知图像质量评估（IQA）最可靠的方法，但其成本高且可扩展性有限，促使人们转向自动化方法。我们研究视觉语言模型（VLMs）是否能在对比度、色彩丰富度和整体偏好三个图像质量维度上近似人类感知判断。六种VLMs（四种专有模型和两种开源模型）与心理物理学数据进行基准比较。本研究通过与人类心理物理学数据对比，系统评估了VLMs在感知IQA中的表现。结果表明，模型在不同属性上表现差异显著：色彩丰富度（ρ高达0.93）的人类对齐度高，但在对比度上表现较差，反之亦然。属性加权分析进一步显示，多数VLMs在评估整体偏好时，对色彩丰富度的权重高于对比度，与心理物理学数据一致。模型内部一致性分析揭示了一个反直觉的权衡：最自洽的模型未必最符合人类判断，表明响应差异反映了对场景依赖感知线索的敏感性。此外，人类与VLM的一致性随感知可区分性增加而提高，表明当刺激差异明显时，VLMs更可靠。

Motivated by the need for scalable alternatives to costly psychophysical experiments in perceptual image quality assessment, this study evaluates whether Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can approximate human judgments across contrast, colorfulness, and overall preference. Six VLMs—four proprietary and two open-weight—are benchmarked against human psychophysical data, revealing that models align strongly with human perception for colorfulness (up to ρ=0.93) but underperform on contrast. Attribute weighting analysis shows VLMs prioritize colorfulness over contrast in preference judgments, mirroring human behavior. Crucially, human-VLM agreement improves when stimuli are perceptually distinct, indicating VLMs are more reliable under clear perceptual differences.
为寻求替代昂贵心理物理实验的可扩展方法，本研究评估了视觉语言模型（VLMs）在对比度、色彩丰富度和整体偏好三个图像质量维度上是否能模拟人类感知判断。六种VLMs（四款专有模型、两款开源模型）与人类心理物理数据对比，结果显示模型在色彩丰富度上表现优异（相关系数最高达0.93），但在对比度上表现较差。属性权重分析表明，VLMs在评估整体偏好时倾向于赋予色彩丰富度更高权重，与人类行为一致。模型内部一致性分析发现，一致性最高的模型未必最贴近人类判断，暗示其响应差异反映了对场景依赖感知线索的敏感性。此外，当刺激差异明显时，人类与VLM的一致性显著提高，表明VLM在感知差异显著时更具可靠性。
EndoVGGT: GNN-Enhanced Depth Estimation for Surgical 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Falong Fan, Yi Xie, Arnis Lektauers, Bo Liu, Jerzy Rozenblit
First: 2026-03-25T17:53:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:53:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate 3D reconstruction of deformable soft tissues is essential for surgical robotic perception. However, low-texture surfaces, specular highlights, and instrument occlusions often fragment geometric continuity, posing a challenge for existing fixed-topology approaches. To address this, we propose EndoVGGT, a geometry-centric framework equipped with a Deformation-aware Graph Attention (DeGAT) module. Rather than using static spatial neighborhoods, DeGAT dynamically constructs feature-space semantic graphs to capture long-range correlations among coherent tissue regions. This enables robust propagation of structural cues across occlusions, enforcing global consistency and improving non-rigid deformation recovery. Extensive experiments on SCARED show that our method significantly improves fidelity, increasing PSNR by 24.6% and SSIM by 9.1% over prior state-of-the-art. Crucially, EndoVGGT exhibits strong zero-shot cross-dataset generalization to the unseen SCARED and EndoNeRF domains, confirming that DeGAT learns domain-agnostic geometric priors. These results highlight the efficacy of dynamic feature-space modeling for consistent surgical 3D reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EndoVGGT：基于图神经网络增强的深度估计用于手术三维重建
准确重建可变形软组织的三维结构对手术机器人感知至关重要。然而，低纹理表面、镜面高光和器械遮挡常破坏几何连续性，给现有固定拓扑方法带来挑战。为此，我们提出EndoVGGT，一种以几何为中心的框架，配备变形感知图注意力（DeGAT）模块。DeGAT摒弃静态空间邻域，动态构建特征空间语义图，捕捉连贯组织区域间的长程关联，从而在遮挡区域稳健传播结构线索，强化全局一致性并提升非刚性形变恢复能力。在SCARED数据集上的大量实验表明，本方法显著提升重建保真度，PSNR提升24.6%，SSIM提升9.1%。更重要的是，EndoVGGT在未见的SCARED和EndoNeRF数据集上展现出强大的零样本跨域泛化能力，证实DeGAT学习到的是与领域无关的几何先验。这些结果凸显了动态特征空间建模在手术三维重建中的一致性优势。
Summary / 总结
To improve 3D reconstruction of soft tissues in surgical settings where textureless surfaces and occlusions disrupt geometric continuity, EndoVGGT introduces a Deformation-aware Graph Attention (DeGAT) module that dynamically constructs semantic feature graphs to capture long-range tissue coherence. This enables robust propagation of structural cues across occlusions, enhancing global consistency and non-rigid deformation recovery. Evaluated on SCARED, EndoVGGT outperforms prior methods with a 24.6% PSNR and 9.1% SSIM gain, and demonstrates strong zero-shot generalization to unseen domains like SCARED and EndoNeRF, confirming its learning of domain-agnostic geometric priors.
为解决手术场景中软组织变形重建时因低纹理表面和遮挡导致的几何连续性断裂问题，EndoVGGT提出一种以几何为中心的框架，配备变形感知图注意力（DeGAT）模块。DeGAT在特征空间中动态构建语义图，捕捉连贯组织区域间的长程关联，实现遮挡区域的结构信息稳健传播与全局一致性。在SCARED数据集上的实验表明，该方法相较现有最先进方法PSNR提升24.6%，SSIM提升9.1%，并在未见的SCARED和EndoNeRF数据集上展现出强大的零样本跨域泛化能力，证明DeGAT学习到的是领域无关的几何先验。
Is Multilingual LLM Watermarking Truly Multilingual? Scaling Robustness to 100+ Languages via Back-Translation
Authors: Asim Mohamed, Martin Gubri
First: 2025-10-20T18:51:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:52:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multilingual watermarking aims to make large language model (LLM) outputs traceable across languages, yet current methods still fall short. Despite claims of cross-lingual robustness, they are evaluated only on high-resource languages. We show that existing multilingual watermarking methods are not truly multilingual: they fail to remain robust under translation attacks in medium- and low-resource languages. We trace this failure to semantic clustering, which fails when the tokenizer vocabulary contains too few full-word tokens for a given language. To address this, we introduce STEAM, a detection method that uses Bayesian optimisation to search among 133 candidate languages for the back-translation that best recovers the watermark strength. It is compatible with any watermarking method, robust across different tokenizers and languages, non-invasive, and easily extendable to new languages. With average gains of +0.23 AUC and +37% TPR@1%, STEAM provides a scalable approach toward fairer watermarking across the diversity of languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多语言大语言模型水印真的多语言吗？通过回译实现100多种语言的鲁棒性扩展
多语言水印旨在使大语言模型（LLM）的输出在不同语言间可追溯，但现有方法仍存在不足。尽管声称具备跨语言鲁棒性，其评估仅限于高资源语言。我们发现现有多语言水印方法并非真正多语言：在中低资源语言下，其在翻译攻击下失效。此失效源于语义聚类，当分词器词汇表中某语言的完整词元过少时，聚类失败。为解决此问题，我们提出STEAM，一种利用贝叶斯优化在133种候选语言中搜索最佳回译以恢复水印强度的检测方法。STEAM兼容任何水印方法，对不同分词器和语言鲁棒，无侵入性，且易于扩展至新语言。其平均AUC提升+0.23，TPR@1%提升37%，为实现跨语言公平水印提供可扩展方案。
Summary / 总结
Multilingual watermarking aims to make large language model (LLM) outputs traceable across languages, yet current methods still fall short.
鉴于现有多语言水印方法在中低资源语言下无法抵御翻译攻击，本文探究其失效原因，发现源于分词器缺乏足够完整词元导致语义聚类失败。为此，作者提出STEAM方法，通过贝叶斯优化在133种候选语言中搜索最佳回译策略以恢复水印强度。该方法兼容任意水印方案、分词器和语言，且无侵入性。实验表明，STEAM平均提升AUC 0.23，TPR@1%提升37%，实现了可扩展、公平的多语言水印检测。
Chameleon: Episodic Memory for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Xinying Guo, Chenxi Jiang, Hyun Bin Kim, Ying Sun, Yang Xiao, Yuhang Han, Jianfei Yang
First: 2026-03-25T17:52:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:52:43+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/gxyes/MARS_Chameleon
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Robotic manipulation often requires memory: occlusion and state changes can make decision-time observations perceptually aliased, making action selection non-Markovian at the observation level because the same observation may arise from different interaction histories. Most embodied agents implement memory via semantically compressed traces and similarity-based retrieval, which discards disambiguating fine-grained perceptual cues and can return perceptually similar but decision-irrelevant episodes. Inspired by human episodic memory, we propose Chameleon, which writes geometry-grounded multimodal tokens to preserve disambiguating context and produces goal-directed recall through a differentiable memory stack. We also introduce Camo-Dataset, a real-robot UR5e dataset spanning episodic recall, spatial tracking, and sequential manipulation under perceptual aliasing. Across tasks, Chameleon consistently improves decision reliability and long-horizon control over strong baselines in perceptually confusable settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：变色龙：面向长时程机器人操作的分段记忆
机器人操作常需记忆：遮挡与状态变化会使决策时刻的观测产生感知歧义，导致在观测层面行动选择非马尔可夫，因相同观测可能源于不同交互历史。多数具身智能体通过语义压缩轨迹与基于相似性的检索实现记忆，但会丢弃区分性细粒度感知线索，可能返回感知相似但决策无关的片段。受人类情景记忆启发，我们提出变色龙，通过几何锚定的多模态令牌记录区分性上下文，并通过可微记忆栈实现目标导向的回忆。同时引入Camo数据集，为真实UR5e机器人提供涵盖情景回忆、空间跟踪与感知歧义下的序列操作的数据。在各类任务中，变色龙在感知混淆场景下持续优于强基线，提升决策可靠性与长时程控制能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that robotic manipulation under occlusion or state changes leads to perceptually aliased observations, making decision-making non-Markovian, the authors propose Chameleon, a memory system inspired by human episodic memory. It encodes geometry-grounded multimodal tokens to preserve fine-grained perceptual context and retrieves relevant episodes via a differentiable memory stack for goal-directed recall. Evaluated on Camo-Dataset—a real-robot UR5e dataset capturing episodic recall, spatial tracking, and sequential manipulation under aliasing—Chameleon consistently outperforms strong baselines by improving decision reliability and long-horizon control in perceptually confusable scenarios.
为了解决机器人操作中因遮挡和状态变化导致的感知别名问题（相同观测可能来自不同交互历史），本文提出Chameleon系统，其灵感来源于人类情景记忆。该系统通过编码几何锚定的多模态令牌，保留细粒度上下文线索，并借助可微分记忆栈实现目标导向的回忆。在Camo-Dataset（一个涵盖情景回忆、空间跟踪和序列操作的现实机器人UR5e数据集）上评估时，Chameleon在感知混淆环境下持续优于多个强基线方法，显著提升了决策可靠性与长时程控制性能。
VFIG: Vectorizing Complex Figures in SVG with Vision-Language Models
Authors: Qijia He, Xunmei Liu, Hammaad Memon, Ziang Li, Zixian Ma, Jaemin Cho, Jason Ren, Daniel S Weld, Ranjay Krishna
First: 2026-03-25T17:52:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:52:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) are an essential format for technical illustration and digital design, offering precise resolution independence and flexible semantic editability. In practice, however, original vector source files are frequently lost or inaccessible, leaving only "flat" rasterized versions (e.g., PNG or JPEG) that are difficult to modify or scale. Manually reconstructing these figures is a prohibitively labor-intensive process, requiring specialized expertise to recover the original geometric intent. To bridge this gap, we propose VFIG, a family of Vision-Language Models trained for complex and high-fidelity figure-to-SVG conversion. While this task is inherently data-driven, existing datasets are typically small-scale and lack the complexity of professional diagrams. We address this by introducing VFIG-DATA, a large-scale dataset of 66K high-quality figure-SVG pairs, curated from a diverse mix of real-world paper figures and procedurally generated diagrams. Recognizing that SVGs are composed of recurring primitives and hierarchical local structures, we introduce a coarse-to-fine training curriculum that begins with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to learn atomic primitives and transitions to reinforcement learning (RL) refinement to optimize global diagram fidelity, layout consistency, and topological edge cases. Finally, we introduce VFIG-BENCH, a comprehensive evaluation suite with novel metrics designed to measure the structural integrity of complex figures. VFIG achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models and performs on par with GPT-5.2, achieving a VLM-Judge score of 0.829 on VFIG-BENCH.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of reconstructing lost or rasterized SVG figures, the authors propose VFIG, a Vision-Language Model family trained to convert complex figures into accurate SVG representations. To overcome data scarcity and complexity gaps, they introduce VFIG-DATA, a 66K-pair dataset combining real-world and procedurally generated diagrams. Training employs a coarse-to-fine curriculum: initial supervised fine-tuning for primitive learning, followed by reinforcement learning to refine global structure, layout, and topology. Evaluated via VFIG-BENCH with novel structural metrics, VFIG outperforms other open-source models and matches GPT-5.2’s performance, achieving a VLM-Judge score of 0.829.
为解决从失传的位图图像重建SVG矢量图的难题，作者提出VFIG模型，专用于复杂图形到高保真SVG的转换。为弥补数据不足，构建了包含6.6万对高质量图-矢量图的VFIG-DATA数据集。训练采用粗到细策略：先用监督微调学习基本图形元素，再用强化学习优化整体布局与拓扑结构。在自研的VFIG-BENCH评估体系中，VFIG超越现有开源模型，性能与GPT-5.2相当，VLM-Judge得分达0.829。
Towards Training-Free Scene Text Editing
Authors: Yubo Li, Xugong Qin, Peng Zhang, Hailun Lin, Gangyan Zeng, Kexin Zhang
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-25T17:50:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:50:31+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Scene text editing seeks to modify textual content in natural images while maintaining visual realism and semantic consistency. Existing methods often require task-specific training or paired data, limiting their scalability and adaptability. In this paper, we propose TextFlow, a training-free scene text editing framework that integrates the strengths of Attention Boost (AttnBoost) and Flow Manifold Steering (FMS) to enable flexible, high-fidelity text manipulation without additional training. Specifically, FMS preserves the structural and style consistency by modeling the visual flow of characters and background regions, while AttnBoost enhances the rendering of textual content through attention-based guidance. By jointly leveraging these complementary modules, our approach performs end-to-end text editing through semantic alignment and spatial refinement in a plug-and-play manner. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves visual quality and text accuracy comparable to or superior to those of training-based counterparts, generalizing well across diverse scenes and languages. This study advances scene text editing toward a more efficient, generalizable, and training-free paradigm. Code is available at https://github.com/lyb18758/TextFlow

Completeness of Unbounded Best-First Minimax and Descent Minimax
Authors: Quentin Cohen-Solal
First: 2026-03-25T17:50:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:50:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this article, we focus on search algorithms for two-player perfect information games, whose objective is to determine the best possible strategy, and ideally a winning strategy. Unfortunately, some search algorithms for games in the literature are not able to always determine a winning strategy, even with an infinite search time. This is the case, for example, of the following algorithms: Unbounded Best-First Minimax and Descent Minimax, which are core algorithms in state-of-the-art knowledge-free reinforcement learning. They were then improved with the so-called completion technique. However, whether this technique sufficiently improves these algorithms to allow them to always determine a winning strategy remained an open question until now. To answer this question, we generalize the two algorithms (their versions using the completion technique), and we show that any algorithm of this class of algorithms computes the best strategy. Finally, we experimentally show that the completion technique improves winning performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无界最佳优先极小极大与下降极小极大算法的完备性
本文聚焦于两人完美信息博弈的搜索算法，目标是确定最优策略，理想情况下为必胜策略。遗憾的是，文献中部分博弈搜索算法即使拥有无限搜索时间，也无法始终确定必胜策略，例如无界最佳优先极小极大与下降极小极大算法——这些是当前知识无监督强化学习的核心算法。随后，人们通过所谓“完备化技术”对其进行了改进，但该技术是否足以使这些算法始终确定必胜策略，此前仍是一个开放问题。为解答此问题，我们对这两类算法（含完备化技术版本）进行推广，并证明此类算法中的任意算法均可计算最优策略。最后，我们通过实验表明完备化技术可提升必胜表现。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether Unbounded Best-First Minimax and Descent Minimax, core algorithms in knowledge-free reinforcement learning for two-player perfect information games, can always determine a winning strategy with infinite search time. Motivated by the unresolved question of whether the completion technique sufficiently enhances these algorithms, the authors generalize both algorithms—including their completion variants—and prove that any algorithm in this class computes the optimal strategy. Experimental results confirm that the completion technique indeed improves winning performance, resolving the open question.
为确保两人零和完美信息博弈中策略的完备性，本文研究了Unbounded Best-First Minimax与Descent Minimax算法是否能在无限搜索时间内始终找到必胜策略。作者将这两类算法（含完成技术改进版本）进行泛化，并证明该类所有算法均可计算最优策略，从而解决了此前关于完成技术是否充分的开放性问题。实验表明，完成技术显著提升了获胜表现。
Anti-I2V: Safeguarding your photos from malicious image-to-video generation
Authors: Duc Vu, Anh Nguyen, Chi Tran, Anh Tran
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-25T17:48:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:48:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026 (Main Conference)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Advances in diffusion-based video generation models, while significantly improving human animation, poses threats of misuse through the creation of fake videos from a specific person's photo and text prompts. Recent efforts have focused on adversarial attacks that introduce crafted perturbations to protect images from diffusion-based models. However, most existing approaches target image generation, while relatively few explicitly address image-to-video diffusion models (VDMs), and most primarily focus on UNet-based architectures. Hence, their effectiveness against Diffusion Transformer (DiT) models remains largely under-explored, as these models demonstrate improved feature retention, and stronger temporal consistency due to larger capacity and advanced attention mechanisms. In this work, we introduce Anti-I2V, a novel defense against malicious human image-to-video generation, applicable across diverse diffusion backbones. Instead of restricting noise updates to the RGB space, Anti-I2V operates in both the $L$*$a$*$b$* and frequency domains, improving robustness and concentrating on salient pixels. We then identify the network layers that capture the most distinct semantic features during the denoising process to design appropriate training objectives that maximize degradation of temporal coherence and generation fidelity. Through extensive validation, Anti-I2V demonstrates state-of-the-art defense performance against diverse video diffusion models, offering an effective solution to the problem.

标题：Anti-I2V：保护您的照片免受恶意图像到视频生成的侵害
基于扩散的视频生成模型虽然显著提升了人类动画效果，但也带来了通过特定人物照片和文本提示生成虚假视频的滥用风险。近期研究主要聚焦于对抗性攻击，通过引入精心设计的扰动来保护图像免受扩散模型的影响。然而，现有大多数方法针对的是图像生成，较少专门针对图像到视频扩散模型（VDMs），且多数仅关注UNet架构。因此，它们对扩散Transformer（DiT）模型的有效性仍被严重忽视，因为这些模型凭借更大的容量和先进的注意力机制，表现出更强的特征保留能力和时间一致性。本文提出Anti-I2V，一种针对恶意人类图像到视频生成的新型防御方法，适用于多种扩散骨干网络。与仅限制噪声更新在RGB空间不同，Anti-I2V同时在Lab和频域操作，提升鲁棒性并聚焦显著像素。我们进一步识别在去噪过程中捕捉最显著语义特征的网络层，设计合适的训练目标以最大化破坏时间一致性与生成保真度。通过广泛验证，Anti-I2V在多种视频扩散模型上展现出最先进的防御性能，为该问题提供有效解决方案。

POLY-SIM: Polyglot Speaker Identification with Missing Modality Grand Challenge 2026 Evaluation Plan
Authors: Marta Moscati, Muhammad Saad Saeed, Marina Zanoni, Mubashir Noman, Rohan Kumar Das, Monorama Swain, Yufang Hou, Elisabeth Andre, Khalid Mahmood Malik, Markus Schedl, Shah Nawaz
Venue: ACM MM 2026
First: 2026-03-25T17:47:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:47:00+00:00
Comments: Grand challenge at ACM MM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal speaker identification systems typically assume the availability of complete and homogeneous audio-visual modalities during both training and testing. However, in real-world applications, such assumptions often do not hold. Visual information may be missing due to occlusions, camera failures, or privacy constraints, while multilingual speakers introduce additional complexity due to linguistic variability across languages. These challenges significantly affect the robustness and generalization of multimodal speaker identification systems. The POLY-SIM Grand Challenge 2026 aims to advance research in multimodal speaker identification under missing-modality and cross-lingual conditions. Specifically, the Grand Challenge encourages the development of robust methods that can effectively leverage incomplete multimodal inputs while maintaining strong performance across different languages. This report presents the design and organization of the POLY-SIM Grand Challenge 2026, including the dataset, task formulation, evaluation protocol, and baseline model. By providing a standardized benchmark and evaluation framework, the challenge aims to foster progress toward more robust and practical multimodal speaker identification systems.

为应对现实场景中多模态说话人识别系统常遇的视觉模态缺失或跨语言问题，POLY-SIM 2026 大赛设计了标准化数据集与评测协议，评估在模态不完整和语言多样条件下仍能保持高性能的识别方法。实验结果显示，表现最佳的系统通过精心设计的模态融合策略和语言感知架构，在视觉缺失或跨语言场景下显著优于基线模型，验证了系统鲁棒性对实际应用的重要性。
Algorithms with Calibrated Machine Learning Predictions
Authors: Judy Hanwen Shen, Ellen Vitercik, Anders Wikum
Venue: ICML 2025
First: 2025-02-05T03:41:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:45:19+00:00
Comments: Matches the camera-ready version accepted at ICML 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The field of algorithms with predictions incorporates machine learning advice in the design of online algorithms to improve real-world performance. A central consideration is the extent to which predictions can be trusted -- while existing approaches often require users to specify an aggregate trust level, modern machine learning models can provide estimates of prediction-level uncertainty. In this paper, we propose calibration as a principled and practical tool to bridge this gap, demonstrating the benefits of calibrated advice through two case studies: the ski rental and online job scheduling problems. For ski rental, we design an algorithm that achieves near-optimal prediction-dependent performance and prove that, in high-variance settings, calibrated advice offers more effective guidance than alternative methods for uncertainty quantification. For job scheduling, we demonstrate that using a calibrated predictor leads to significant performance improvements over existing methods. Evaluations on real-world data validate our theoretical findings, highlighting the practical impact of calibration for algorithms with predictions.

In ski rental, the calibrated algorithm achieves near-optimal prediction-dependent performance and outperforms alternatives in high-variance settings. In job scheduling, calibrated predictors yield substantial performance gains over existing methods, with real-world evaluations confirming the theoretical advantages of calibration.
为提升在线算法在机器学习预测中的实际表现并有效处理预测不确定性，本文提出校准方法，将预测层面的不确定性转化为可操作的算法指导。通过滑雪租赁与在线任务调度两个案例，作者证明校准预测在滑雪租赁中可实现近最优性能，在任务调度中显著优于非校准方法。真实数据实验验证了理论发现，表明校准在高方差环境下能有效提升算法决策质量，优于传统信任水平设定方法。
MiniBEE: A New Form Factor for Compact Bimanual Dexterity
Authors: Sharfin Islam, Zewen Chen, Zhanpeng He, Swapneel Bhatt, Andres Permuy, Brock Taylor, James Vickery, Zhengbin Lu, Cheng Zhang, Pedro Piacenza, Matei Ciocarlie
First: 2025-10-02T02:37:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:44:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bimanual robot manipulators can achieve impressive dexterity, but typically rely on two full six- or seven- degree-of-freedom arms so that paired grippers can coordinate effectively. This traditional framework increases system complexity while only exploiting a fraction of the overall workspace for dexterous interaction. We introduce the MiniBEE (Miniature Bimanual End-effector), a compact system in which two reduced-mobility arms (3+ DOF each) are coupled into a kinematic chain that preserves full relative positioning between grippers. To guide our design, we formulate a kinematic dexterity metric that enlarges the dexterous workspace while keeping the mechanism lightweight and wearable. The resulting system supports two complementary modes: (i) wearable kinesthetic data collection with self-tracked gripper poses, and (ii) deployment on a standard robot arm, extending dexterity across its entire workspace. We present kinematic analysis and design optimization methods for maximizing dexterous range, and demonstrate an end-to-end pipeline in which wearable demonstrations train imitation learning policies that perform robust, real-world bimanual manipulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MiniBEE：一种紧凑型双臂灵巧操作的新形态

Experimental results show that MiniBEE achieves robust bimanual manipulation through imitation learning trained on wearable demonstrations, demonstrating effective dexterity across real-world tasks.
为解决传统双臂机器人系统复杂且工作空间利用率低的问题，作者提出MiniBEE，一种由两个轻量级3+自由度手臂通过运动链连接的紧凑型双臂末端执行器，保持夹爪相对位置不变。通过设计运动灵巧性指标优化结构，实现最大灵巧工作空间与可穿戴性兼顾。实验表明，MiniBEE可通过穿戴式演示训练模仿学习策略，实现鲁棒的现实世界双臂操作，支持自跟踪数据采集及在标准机械臂上部署以扩展工作空间灵巧性。
Trust Region Constrained Bayesian Optimization with Penalized Constraint Handling
Authors: Raju Chowdhury, Tanmay Sen, Prajamitra Bhuyan, Biswabrata Pradhan
First: 2026-03-25T17:44:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:44:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Constrained optimization in high-dimensional black-box settings is difficult due to expensive evaluations, the lack of gradient information, and complex feasibility regions. In this work, we propose a Bayesian optimization method that combines a penalty formulation, a surrogate model, and a trust region strategy. The constrained problem is converted to an unconstrained form by penalizing constraint violations, which provides a unified modeling framework. A trust region restricts the search to a local region around the current best solution, which improves stability and efficiency in high dimensions. Within this region, we use the Expected Improvement acquisition function to select evaluation points by balancing improvement and uncertainty. The proposed Trust Region method integrates penalty-based constraint handling with local surrogate modeling. This combination enables efficient exploration of feasible regions while maintaining sample efficiency. We compare the proposed method with state-of-the-art methods on synthetic and real-world high-dimensional constrained optimization problems. The results show that the method identifies high-quality feasible solutions with fewer evaluations and maintains stable performance across different settings.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of constrained optimization in high-dimensional black-box settings—such as expensive evaluations, lack of gradients, and complex feasibility regions—the paper proposes a Bayesian optimization method that integrates a penalty-based constraint handling scheme, a surrogate model, and a trust region strategy. The method reformulates the constrained problem into an unconstrained one via penalty terms, then confines search to a local region around the current best solution using a trust region to enhance stability and efficiency. Within this region, it employs the Expected Improvement acquisition function to balance exploration and exploitation. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world high-dimensional problems demonstrate that the method consistently identifies high-quality feasible solutions with fewer evaluations compared to state-of-the-art approaches.
为应对高维黑箱约束优化中评估成本高、无梯度信息及可行域复杂等挑战，本文提出一种结合惩罚式约束处理、代理模型与信任区域策略的贝叶斯优化方法。该方法通过惩罚违反约束的解，将约束问题转化为无约束形式，实现统一建模。信任区域限制搜索范围在当前最优解附近，提升稳定性和效率。区域内采用期望改进采样函数平衡探索与利用。实验结果表明，该方法在合成与真实高维约束问题上，以更少评估次数找到高质量可行解，并在不同设置下保持稳定表现。
Infrastructure for Valuable, Tradable, and Verifiable Agent Memory
Authors: Mengyuan Li, Lei Gao, Haoxuan Xu, Jiate Li, Potung Yu, Lingke Cheng, Yue Zhao, Murali Annavaram
First: 2026-03-25T17:43:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:43:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Every API token you spend is your accumulated wealth; once you can prove its value and the effort behind it, you can resell it. As autonomous agents repeatedly call models and tools, they accumulate memories that are your intellectual property. But today these memories remain private and non-transferable, as there is no way to validate their value. We argue that agent memory can serve as an economic commodity in the agent economy, if buyers can verify that it is authentic, effort-backed, and produced in a compatible execution context. To realize this idea, we propose clawgang, which binds memory to verifiable computational provenance, and meowtrade, a market layer for listing, transferring, and governing certified memory artifacts. Together, they transform one-shot API token spending into reusable and tradable assets, enabling timely memory transfer, reducing repeated exploration, and opening a memory trade market.

Scaling Recurrence-aware Foundation Models for Clinical Records via Next-Visit Prediction
Authors: Haresh Rengaraj Rajamohan, Xiang Gao, Weicheng Zhu, Shih-Lun Huang, Long Chen, Gabe Schulman, Huizhen Jin, Shengduo Li, Yixuan Wang, Huidi Yang, Kyunghyun Cho, Cem M. Deniz, Narges Razavian
First: 2026-03-25T17:42:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-25T17:42:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While large-scale pretraining has revolutionized language modeling, its potential remains underexplored in healthcare with structured electronic health records (EHRs). We present RAVEN, a novel generative pretraining strategy for sequential EHR data based on Recurrence-Aware next-Visit EveNt prediction. Leveraging a dataset of over one million unique individuals, our model learns to autoregressively generate tokenized clinical events for the next visit conditioned on patient history. We introduce regularization on predicting repeated events and highlight a key pitfall in EHR-based foundation model evaluations: repeated event tokens can inflate performance metrics when new onsets are not distinguished from subsequent occurrences. Furthermore, we empirically investigate the scaling behaviors in a data-constrained, compute-saturated regime, showing that simply increasing model size is suboptimal without commensurate increases in data volume. We evaluate our model via zero-shot prediction for forecasting the incidence of a diverse set of diseases, where it rivals fully fine-tuned representation-based Transformer models and outperforms widely used simulation-based next-token approaches. Finally, without additional parameter updates, we show that RAVEN can generalize to an external patient cohort under lossy clinical code mappings and feature coverage gaps.

标题：通过下一次就诊预测扩展具有复发感知能力的临床记录基础模型
尽管大规模预训练已革新语言建模，但在医疗领域，其在结构化电子健康记录（EHR）中的潜力尚未充分探索。我们提出了RAVEN，一种基于复发感知下一次就诊事件预测的新型生成预训练策略。利用超过一百万独特个体的数据集，我们的模型能够根据患者历史自回归生成下一就诊的标记化临床事件。我们引入了对重复事件预测的正则化，并指出了基于EHR的基础模型评估中的一个关键陷阱：当新发事件未与后续复发区分时，重复事件标记会虚高性能指标。此外，我们在数据受限、计算饱和的环境下实证研究了模型的扩展行为，表明仅增加模型规模而不相应增加数据量是次优的。我们通过零样本预测评估模型对多种疾病发生率的预测能力，结果表明其可媲美完全微调的基于表示的Transformer模型，且优于广泛使用的基于模拟的下一标记方法。最后，在无需额外参数更新的情况下，我们证明RAVEN可在临床编码映射丢失和特征覆盖缺失的外部患者队列中实现泛化。
